## Implementation Plan: Auto-Clean Orphaned Cron Sessions + Auto-Prune Sandbox Containers

### Current State Summary

**Session keys for cron jobs follow this pattern** (in `server.rs` lines 1951-1955):
- Named sessions: `cron:{name}` (e.g., `cron:heartbeat`)
- Isolated sessions: `cron:{uuid}` (unique per run)

**Key issue**: Isolated cron sessions (`SessionTarget::Isolated`) generate a unique UUID-based session key each run. These accumulate as JSONL files in `<data_dir>/agents/<agentId>/sessions/` and as rows in the `sessions` SQLite table. Neither the session metadata nor the cron run record stores a link back to the cron job that created them, and there is no existing retention/cleanup mechanism.

**Sandbox containers** are created per-session via `SandboxRouter`. The gateway cleans up sandbox overrides after each cron run (lines 2018-2022), but the actual container persists until the next gateway restart (startup GC at line 2375-2391). There is no post-run container pruning.

---

### Feature 1: Auto-Clean Orphaned Cron Sessions

#### 1.1 Add `session_key` to `CronRunRecord` and `AgentTurnResult`

The core problem is that there is no link between a cron run and the session it created. We need to thread the session key from the gateway's `on_agent_turn` callback back into the cron run record.

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/service.rs`**
- Add `session_key: Option<String>` to `AgentTurnResult` (line 29-33)

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/types.rs`**
- Add `session_key: Option<String>` to `CronRunRecord` (line 131-145), with `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]` for backward compatibility

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/gateway/src/server.rs`**
- In the `on_agent_turn` closure (around line 2047), return `session_key: Some(session_key)` in the `AgentTurnResult`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/service.rs`** (execute_job, around line 604)
- Populate `session_key` from the `AgentTurnResult` into the `CronRunRecord`

#### 1.2 Add `session_key` column to `cron_runs` table

**New migration file: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/migrations/20260406100000_cron_runs_session_key.sql`**
```sql
ALTER TABLE cron_runs ADD COLUMN session_key TEXT;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cron_runs_session_key ON cron_runs(session_key);
```

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs`**
- Update `append_run` to bind `session_key` (line 97-114)
- Update `get_runs` query and row mapping to include `session_key` (line 117-157)

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_file.rs`**
- Backward-compatible: `session_key` is in the JSON via serde, so file store needs no structural changes (it serializes the full `CronRunRecord`)

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_memory.rs`**
- Same: serde-based, no structural changes needed

#### 1.3 Add Config: `cron.session_retention_days`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/config/src/schema.rs`**
- Add `session_retention_days: Option<u64>` to `CronConfig` (around line 964). Default `Some(7)`. `None` means disabled.

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/config/src/validate.rs`**
- Add `"session_retention_days"` as a `Leaf` under the `"cron"` entry in `build_schema_map()` (around line 532-536)

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/config/src/template.rs`**
- Add commented `session_retention_days` entry in the `[cron]` section

#### 1.4 Implement Session Retention Pruning

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/service.rs`**
- Add a new method `prune_old_sessions` (or better, a new module)
- Add `on_delete_session: Option<DeleteSessionFn>` callback type to `CronService` (similar to the existing `on_agent_turn` / `on_system_event` pattern)
- Add a new callback type: `pub type DeleteSessionFn = Arc<dyn Fn(String) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<()>> + Send>> + Send + Sync>;`
- This callback is invoked by the cron service to delete a session by key

**Alternative (and better) approach**: Implement the pruning as a periodic task in the gateway rather than in the cron crate itself, since the gateway owns both the session store and the cron service. This keeps the cron crate focused and avoids circular dependencies.

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/gateway/src/server.rs`**
- After cron service start (around line 3340), spawn a background task that runs every hour:
  1. Query `sessions` table for rows where `key LIKE 'cron:%'` and `updated_at < now - retention_days * 86400000`
  2. For each such session: delete the JSONL file via `SessionStore::clear()`, remove the metadata entry via `SqliteSessionMetadata::remove()`, and clean up the sandbox container via `SandboxRouter::cleanup_session()`
  3. Also prune old `cron_runs` rows older than the retention period (to keep the database clean)

This is the cleanest approach because:
- The gateway already has references to `SessionStore`, `SqliteSessionMetadata`, `SandboxRouter`, and the config
- No new callback types needed in the cron crate
- The pruning is a gateway concern (session + sandbox lifecycle), not a scheduling concern

**New helper function in gateway**: `async fn prune_cron_sessions(...)` that:
1. Lists sessions with keys starting with `cron:` and `updated_at` older than retention threshold
2. Excludes named cron sessions that are still active (i.e., the corresponding cron job still exists and uses `SessionTarget::Named`)
3. Deletes the session file, metadata row, and sandbox container for each expired session
4. Optionally prunes old `cron_runs` rows via a new `CronStore::prune_runs_before(timestamp)` method

#### 1.5 Add `prune_runs_before` to CronStore trait

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store.rs`**
- Add method: `async fn prune_runs_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<u64>;` with a default implementation returning `Ok(0)` for backward compatibility

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs`**
- Implement: `DELETE FROM cron_runs WHERE started_at_ms < ?`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_file.rs`**
- Implement by reading each job's runs JSONL, filtering, and rewriting

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_memory.rs`**
- Implement by filtering the in-memory vec

### Feature 2: Auto-Prune Sandbox Containers After Cron Completion

#### 2.1 Add `auto_prune_container` to `CronSandboxConfig`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/types.rs`**
- Add `auto_prune_container: bool` to `CronSandboxConfig` (line 148-166), defaulting to `true`
- This makes sandbox container pruning the default for cron jobs (they are typically ephemeral)
- Also add to `CronJobCreate` and `CronJobPatch` for API completeness

#### 2.2 Thread `auto_prune_container` through `AgentTurnRequest`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/service.rs`**
- The `AgentTurnRequest` already includes `sandbox: CronSandboxConfig` (line 114), so the field is automatically available

#### 2.3 Prune container after cron job completion in the gateway

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/gateway/src/server.rs`**
- In the `on_agent_turn` closure, after the `send_sync` call completes and sandbox overrides are cleaned up (around lines 2018-2022), add:
  ```rust
  // Auto-prune sandbox container if configured.
  if req.sandbox.enabled && req.sandbox.auto_prune_container {
      if let Some(ref router) = state.sandbox_router {
          if let Err(e) = router.cleanup_session(&session_key).await {
              tracing::debug!(session_key, error = %e, "cron sandbox container cleanup failed");
          }
      }
  }
  ```
- The `SandboxRouter::cleanup_session` method (line 538 of router.rs) already handles: calling `backend.cleanup(id)`, removing overrides, and clearing prepared sessions. This is exactly what we need.

#### 2.4 "No AI output after X time" idle detection

The discussion mentions detecting "no AI output after X time" as a trigger. This is more nuanced and involves the agent turn timeout:

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/types.rs`**
- The `CronPayload::AgentTurn` already has `timeout_secs: Option<u64>` which handles the maximum duration
- The `CronService::execute_job` already handles timeouts and marks runs as errors

For the "idle" detection scenario (where the LLM is done but the container is still running), the implementation in 2.3 already covers this because we prune after the agent turn completes (success or failure). The container cleanup happens as part of the normal post-completion flow.

**No additional idle timer needed**: The agent turn is synchronous from the cron service's perspective -- it awaits `chat.send_sync(params)`. Once that returns, the cron job is "done" and we prune. If the job times out, the existing timeout mechanism in `execute_job` (the `STUCK_THRESHOLD_MS` constant or the per-job `timeout_secs`) will fire, and the cleanup path still runs.

#### 2.5 Global config for default auto-prune behavior

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/config/src/schema.rs`**
- Add `auto_prune_cron_containers: bool` to `CronConfig` with `#[serde(default = "default_true")]`, defaulting to `true`

**File: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/config/src/validate.rs`**
- Add `"auto_prune_cron_containers"` as a `Leaf` under `"cron"` in `build_schema_map()`

The per-job `CronSandboxConfig.auto_prune_container` should use the global default if not explicitly set. Change it from `bool` to `Option<bool>` and resolve at execution time:
```rust
let should_prune = req.sandbox.auto_prune_container
    .unwrap_or(config.cron.auto_prune_cron_containers);
```

### Test Strategy

#### Unit Tests

1. **`crates/cron/src/types.rs`**: Serde roundtrip tests for `CronRunRecord` with `session_key`, `CronSandboxConfig` with `auto_prune_container`, backward compatibility with missing fields
2. **`crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs`**: Test `append_run`/`get_runs` with `session_key`, test `prune_runs_before`
3. **`crates/cron/src/store_file.rs`**: Test `prune_runs_before`
4. **`crates/cron/src/store_memory.rs`**: Test `prune_runs_before`
5. **`crates/config/src/validate.rs`**: Update existing schema completeness test (the test at line 2336 that checks all fields are in `build_schema_map()`)

#### Integration Tests

1. **Gateway cron session pruning**: Mock a cron run that creates a session, verify the session is cleaned up after the retention period
2. **Gateway sandbox auto-prune**: Mock a cron run with `auto_prune_container: true`, verify `cleanup_session` is called after completion
3. **Backward compatibility**: Ensure old cron run records (without `session_key`) deserialize correctly

#### E2E Tests (not strictly required for backend-only changes, but recommended)

If there's a UI component for cron settings, add tests for the new retention/auto-prune config fields.

1. **Phase 1 - Data model** (no behavior change):
   - Add `session_key` to `CronRunRecord` and `AgentTurnResult`
   - Add `auto_prune_container` to `CronSandboxConfig`
   - Add migration for `session_key` column
   - Update store implementations

2. **Phase 2 - Thread session_key through**:

   - Populate it in `CronRunRecord` in `execute_job`

3. **Phase 3 - Auto-prune containers**:
   - Add sandbox cleanup after cron agent turn in gateway
   - Respect `auto_prune_container` setting

4. **Phase 4 - Session retention**:
   - Add `prune_runs_before` to `CronStore` trait + implementations
   - Implement periodic pruning task in gateway
   - Query cron-prefixed sessions, delete expired ones

5. **Phase 5 - Tests and docs**:
   - Unit tests for all new types and methods
   - Integration tests for pruning logic
   - Update docs if there's a cron section in `docs/src/`

Here's the implementation plan for discussion #538 — auto-cleaning orphaned cron sessions and auto-pruning sandbox containers.

## Problem

Cron jobs with `SessionTarget::Isolated` create a new UUID-based session each run. These accumulate as JSONL files + `sessions` table rows with no cleanup. Sandbox containers also persist until gateway restart.

## Plan: 5 Phases

### Phase 1 — Data Model (no behavior change)

| Change | File |
|--------|------|
| Add `session_key: Option<String>` to `CronRunRecord` | `crates/cron/src/types.rs` |
| Add `session_key: Option<String>` to `AgentTurnResult` | `crates/cron/src/service.rs` |
| Add `auto_prune_container: Option<bool>` to `CronSandboxConfig` | `crates/cron/src/types.rs` |
| Migration: `ALTER TABLE cron_runs ADD COLUMN session_key TEXT` | `crates/cron/migrations/` |
| Update SQLite store to read/write `session_key` | `crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs` |
| Add `session_retention_days: Option<u64>` (default 7) + `auto_prune_cron_containers: bool` (default true) to `CronConfig` | `crates/config/src/schema.rs` |
| Update `build_schema_map()` | `crates/config/src/validate.rs` |
| Add commented config entries | `crates/config/src/template.rs` |

### Phase 2 — Thread session_key

- Gateway's `on_agent_turn` closure returns `session_key` in `AgentTurnResult`
- `execute_job` populates it in `CronRunRecord` before saving

### Phase 3 — Auto-prune containers after cron completion

After `send_sync` completes in the `on_agent_turn` callback, call `SandboxRouter::cleanup_session(&session_key)` if `auto_prune_container` resolves to true (per-job setting falls back to global `auto_prune_cron_containers`).

### Phase 4 — Periodic session retention pruning

- Add `prune_runs_before(timestamp)` to `CronStore` trait + all 3 implementations (sqlite, file, memory)
- Spawn a background task in the gateway (runs hourly) that:
  1. Finds sessions where key starts with `cron:` and `updated_at` is older than `retention_days`
  2. Skips named sessions for still-active cron jobs
  3. Deletes session file, metadata row, and sandbox container
  4. Prunes old `cron_runs` rows

### Phase 5 — Tests & docs

- Serde roundtrip tests for backward compat (missing `session_key` in old records)
- Store tests for `prune_runs_before`
- Integration tests for session pruning and container auto-prune
- Config validation completeness tests
- Update cron docs

## Key Design Decisions

1. **Pruning lives in the gateway**, not the cron crate — the gateway owns session stores, sandbox router, and config. Avoids circular deps.
2. **`auto_prune_container` defaults to `true`** — cron containers are ephemeral by nature; opt-out is safer than opt-in.
3. **No idle timer needed** — the agent turn is synchronous from cron's perspective. Once `send_sync` returns, we prune. Timeouts already handled by `timeout_secs`.
4. **Named sessions excluded from retention pruning** — they're intentionally reused across runs.

     1→//! Core data types for the cron scheduling system.
     2→
     3→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     4→
     5→/// Whether to wake the heartbeat after a cron job completes.
     6→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
     7→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
     8→pub enum CronWakeMode {
     9→    /// Trigger an immediate heartbeat after this job fires.
    10→    Now,
    11→    /// Wait for the next scheduled heartbeat tick (default).
    12→    #[default]
    13→    NextHeartbeat,
    14→}
    15→
    16→/// How a job is scheduled.
    17→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
    18→#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "camelCase")]
    19→pub enum CronSchedule {
    20→    /// One-shot: fire once at `at_ms` (epoch millis).
    21→    At { at_ms: u64 },
    22→    /// Fixed interval: fire every `every_ms` millis, optionally anchored.
    23→    Every {
    24→        every_ms: u64,
    25→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    26→        anchor_ms: Option<u64>,
    27→    },
    28→    /// Cron expression (5-field standard or 6-field with seconds).
    29→    Cron {
    30→        expr: String,
    31→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    32→        tz: Option<String>,
    33→    },
    34→}
    35→
    36→/// What happens when a job fires.
    37→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
    38→#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "camelCase")]
    39→pub enum CronPayload {
    40→    /// Inject a system event into the main session.
    41→    SystemEvent { text: String },
    42→    /// Run an isolated agent turn.
    43→    AgentTurn {
    44→        message: String,
    45→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    46→        model: Option<String>,
    47→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    48→        timeout_secs: Option<u64>,
    49→        #[serde(default)]
    50→        deliver: bool,
    51→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    52→        channel: Option<String>,
    53→        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    54→        to: Option<String>,
    55→    },
    56→}
    57→
    58→/// Where the job executes.
    59→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
    60→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
    61→pub enum SessionTarget {
    62→    /// Inject into the main conversation session.
    63→    Main,
    64→    /// Run in an isolated, throwaway session.
    65→    #[default]
    66→    Isolated,
    67→    /// Run in a named session that persists across runs (e.g. "heartbeat").
    68→    Named(String),
    69→}
    70→
    71→/// Outcome of a single job run.
    72→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
    73→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
    74→pub enum RunStatus {
    75→    Ok,
    76→    Error,
    77→    Skipped,
    78→}
    79→
    80→/// Mutable runtime state of a job.
    81→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
    82→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
    83→pub struct CronJobState {
    84→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    85→    pub next_run_at_ms: Option<u64>,
    86→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    87→    pub running_at_ms: Option<u64>,
    88→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    89→    pub last_run_at_ms: Option<u64>,
    90→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    91→    pub last_status: Option<RunStatus>,
    92→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    93→    pub last_error: Option<String>,
    94→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    95→    pub last_duration_ms: Option<u64>,
    96→}
    97→
    98→/// A scheduled cron job.
    99→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   100→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   101→pub struct CronJob {
   102→    pub id: String,
   103→    pub name: String,
   104→    #[serde(default)]
   105→    pub enabled: bool,
   106→    #[serde(default)]
   107→    pub delete_after_run: bool,
   108→    pub schedule: CronSchedule,
   109→    pub payload: CronPayload,
   110→    #[serde(default)]
   111→    pub session_target: SessionTarget,
   112→    #[serde(default)]
   113→    pub state: CronJobState,
   114→    /// Sandbox configuration for this job.
   115→    #[serde(default)]
   116→    pub sandbox: CronSandboxConfig,
   117→    /// Whether to wake the heartbeat after this job completes.
   118→    #[serde(default)]
   119→    pub wake_mode: CronWakeMode,
   120→    /// Whether this is a system-managed job (e.g. heartbeat). System jobs are
   121→    /// hidden from the normal jobs table in the UI.
   122→    #[serde(default)]
   123→    pub system: bool,
   124→    pub created_at_ms: u64,
   125→    pub updated_at_ms: u64,
   126→}
   127→
   128→/// Record of a completed run, stored in run history.
   129→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   130→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   131→pub struct CronRunRecord {
   132→    pub job_id: String,
   133→    pub started_at_ms: u64,
   134→    pub finished_at_ms: u64,
   135→    pub status: RunStatus,
   136→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   137→    pub error: Option<String>,
   138→    pub duration_ms: u64,
   139→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   140→    pub output: Option<String>,
   141→    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   142→    pub input_tokens: Option<u64>,
   143→    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   144→    pub output_tokens: Option<u64>,
   145→}
   146→
   147→/// Sandbox configuration for a cron job.
   148→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   149→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   150→pub struct CronSandboxConfig {
   151→    /// Whether to run the job inside a sandbox. Defaults to true.
   152→    #[serde(default = "default_true")]
   153→    pub enabled: bool,
   154→    /// Override the sandbox image. If `None`, uses the default image.
   155→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   156→    pub image: Option<String>,
   157→}
   158→
   159→impl Default for CronSandboxConfig {
   160→    fn default() -> Self {
   161→        Self {
   162→            enabled: true,
   163→            image: None,
   164→        }
   165→    }
   166→}
   167→
   168→/// Input for creating a new job.
   169→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   170→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   171→pub struct CronJobCreate {
   172→    /// Optional ID for the job. If not provided, a UUID will be generated.
   173→    /// Use a fixed ID for system jobs to preserve run history across restarts.
   174→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   175→    pub id: Option<String>,
   176→    pub name: String,
   177→    pub schedule: CronSchedule,
   178→    pub payload: CronPayload,
   179→    #[serde(default)]
   180→    pub session_target: SessionTarget,
   181→    #[serde(default)]
   182→    pub delete_after_run: bool,
   183→    #[serde(default = "default_true")]
   184→    pub enabled: bool,
   185→    #[serde(default)]
   186→    pub system: bool,
   187→    #[serde(default)]
   188→    pub sandbox: CronSandboxConfig,
   189→    #[serde(default)]
   190→    pub wake_mode: CronWakeMode,
   191→}
   192→
   193→fn default_true() -> bool {
   194→    true
   195→}
   196→
   197→/// Patch for updating an existing job.
   198→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
   199→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   200→pub struct CronJobPatch {
   201→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   202→    pub name: Option<String>,
   203→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   204→    pub schedule: Option<CronSchedule>,
   205→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   206→    pub payload: Option<CronPayload>,
   207→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   208→    pub session_target: Option<SessionTarget>,
   209→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   210→    pub enabled: Option<bool>,
   211→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   212→    pub delete_after_run: Option<bool>,
   213→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   214→    pub sandbox: Option<CronSandboxConfig>,
   215→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   216→    pub wake_mode: Option<CronWakeMode>,
   217→}
   218→
   219→/// Summary status of the cron system.
   220→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   221→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   222→pub struct CronStatus {
   223→    pub running: bool,
   224→    pub job_count: usize,
   225→    pub enabled_count: usize,
   226→    pub next_run_at_ms: Option<u64>,
   227→}
   228→
   229→/// Notification emitted when cron jobs change.
   230→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   231→#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "camelCase")]
   232→pub enum CronNotification {
   233→    /// A new job was created.
   234→    Created { job: CronJob },
   235→    /// An existing job was updated.
   236→    Updated { job: CronJob },
   237→    /// A job was removed.
   238→    Removed { job_id: String },
   239→}

   242→#[cfg(test)]
   243→mod tests {
   244→    use super::*;
   245→
   246→    #[test]
   247→    fn test_schedule_roundtrip_at() {
   248→        let s = CronSchedule::At { at_ms: 1234567890 };
   249→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&s).unwrap();
   250→        let back: CronSchedule = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   251→        assert_eq!(s, back);
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    #[test]
   255→    fn test_schedule_roundtrip_every() {
   256→        let s = CronSchedule::Every {
   257→            every_ms: 60_000,
   258→            anchor_ms: Some(1000),
   259→        };
   260→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&s).unwrap();
   261→        let back: CronSchedule = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   262→        assert_eq!(s, back);
   263→    }
   264→
   265→    #[test]
   266→    fn test_schedule_roundtrip_cron() {
   267→        let s = CronSchedule::Cron {

   270→        };
   271→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&s).unwrap();

   334→    #[test]
   335→    fn test_run_record_roundtrip() {
   336→        let rec = CronRunRecord {

   338→            started_at_ms: 1000,

   349→        assert_eq!(rec, back);

   444→            session_target: SessionTarget::Isolated,
   445→            state: CronJobState::default(),
   446→            sandbox: CronSandboxConfig {
   447→                enabled: false,
   448→                image: Some("my-image:v1".into()),
   449→            },
   450→            wake_mode: CronWakeMode::default(),
   451→            system: false,
   452→            created_at_ms: 1000,
   453→            updated_at_ms: 1000,
   454→        };
   455→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&job).unwrap();
   456→        let back: CronJob = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   457→        assert_eq!(job, back);
   458→        assert!(!back.sandbox.enabled);
   459→        assert_eq!(back.sandbox.image.as_deref(), Some("my-image:v1"));
   460→    }

   462→    #[test]
   463→    fn test_cron_status_serialize() {
   464→        let s = CronStatus {
   465→            running: true,
   466→            job_count: 5,
   467→            enabled_count: 3,
   468→            next_run_at_ms: Some(999),
   469→        };
   470→        let v = serde_json::to_value(&s).unwrap();
   471→        assert_eq!(v["running"], true);

   475→    #[test]
   476→    fn test_wake_mode_serde_roundtrip() {
   477→        let now = CronWakeMode::Now;
   478→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&now).unwrap();
   479→        assert_eq!(json, "\"now\"");
   480→        let back: CronWakeMode = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   481→        assert_eq!(back, CronWakeMode::Now);
   482→
   483→        let next = CronWakeMode::NextHeartbeat;
   484→        let json = serde_json::to_string(&next).unwrap();
   485→        assert_eq!(json, "\"nextHeartbeat\"");
   486→        let back: CronWakeMode = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   487→        assert_eq!(back, CronWakeMode::NextHeartbeat);
   488→    }

   491→    fn test_wake_mode_default() {

   493→    }

   496→    fn test_wake_mode_backward_compat_missing_field() {

   518→        let json = r#"{

   520→            "schedule": { "kind": "at", "at_ms": 1000 },
   521→            "payload": { "kind": "systemEvent", "text": "hi" },
   522→            "wakeMode": "now"

     1→//! Persistence trait and implementations for cron jobs.
     2→
     3→use async_trait::async_trait;

    10→/// Persistence backend for cron jobs and run history.
    11→#[async_trait]
    12→pub trait CronStore: Send + Sync {
    13→    async fn load_jobs(&self) -> Result<Vec<CronJob>>;
    14→    async fn save_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()>;
    15→    async fn delete_job(&self, id: &str) -> Result<()>;
    16→    async fn update_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()>;
    17→    async fn append_run(&self, job_id: &str, run: &CronRunRecord) -> Result<()>;
    18→    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>>;

     1→//! SQLite-backed cron store using sqlx.
     2→
     3→use {
     4→    async_trait::async_trait,
     5→    sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool, sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions},
     6→};
     7→
     8→use crate::{
     9→    Error, Result,
    10→    store::CronStore,
    11→    types::{CronJob, CronRunRecord},
    12→};
    13→
    14→/// SQLite-backed persistence for cron jobs and run history.
    15→pub struct SqliteStore {
    16→    pool: SqlitePool,
    17→}
    18→
    19→impl SqliteStore {
    20→    /// Create a new store with its own connection pool and run migrations.
    21→    ///
    22→    /// Use this for standalone cron databases. For shared pools (e.g., moltis.db),
    23→    /// use [`SqliteStore::with_pool`] after calling [`crate::run_migrations`].
    24→    pub async fn new(database_url: &str) -> Result<Self> {
    25→        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
    26→            .max_connections(5)
    27→            .connect(database_url)
    28→            .await
    29→            .map_err(|source| Error::external("failed to connect to SQLite", source))?;
    30→
    31→        crate::run_migrations(&pool).await?;
    32→
    33→        Ok(Self { pool })
    34→    }
    35→
    36→    /// Create a store using an existing pool (migrations must already be run).
    37→    ///
    38→    /// Call [`crate::run_migrations`] before using this constructor.
    39→    pub fn with_pool(pool: SqlitePool) -> Self {
    40→        Self { pool }
    41→    }
    42→}
    43→
    44→#[async_trait]
    45→impl CronStore for SqliteStore {
    46→    async fn load_jobs(&self) -> Result<Vec<CronJob>> {
    47→        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT data FROM cron_jobs")
    48→            .fetch_all(&self.pool)
    49→            .await?;
    50→
    51→        let mut jobs = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());
    52→        for row in rows {
    53→            let data: String = row.get("data");
    54→            let job: CronJob = serde_json::from_str(&data)?;
    55→            jobs.push(job);
    56→        }
    57→        Ok(jobs)
    58→    }
    59→
    60→    async fn save_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
    61→        let data = serde_json::to_string(job)?;
    62→        sqlx::query(
    63→            "INSERT INTO cron_jobs (id, data) VALUES (?, ?)
    64→             ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET data = excluded.data",
    65→        )
    66→        .bind(&job.id)
    67→        .bind(&data)
    68→        .execute(&self.pool)
    69→        .await?;
    70→        Ok(())
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    async fn delete_job(&self, id: &str) -> Result<()> {
    74→        let result = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM cron_jobs WHERE id = ?")
    75→            .bind(id)
    76→            .execute(&self.pool)
    77→            .await?;
    78→        if result.rows_affected() == 0 {
    79→            return Err(Error::job_not_found(id));
    80→        }
    81→        Ok(())
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    async fn update_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
    85→        let data = serde_json::to_string(job)?;
    86→        let result = sqlx::query("UPDATE cron_jobs SET data = ? WHERE id = ?")
    87→            .bind(&data)
    88→            .bind(&job.id)
    89→            .execute(&self.pool)
    90→            .await?;
    91→        if result.rows_affected() == 0 {
    92→            return Err(Error::job_not_found(job.id.clone()));
    93→        }
    94→        Ok(())
    95→    }
    96→
    97→    async fn append_run(&self, job_id: &str, run: &CronRunRecord) -> Result<()> {
    98→        let status = serde_json::to_string(&run.status)?;
    99→        sqlx::query(
   100→            "INSERT INTO cron_runs (job_id, started_at_ms, finished_at_ms, status, error, duration_ms, output, input_tokens, output_tokens)
   101→             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   102→        )
   103→        .bind(job_id)
   104→        .bind(run.started_at_ms as i64)
   105→        .bind(run.finished_at_ms as i64)
   106→        .bind(&status)
   107→        .bind(&run.error)
   108→        .bind(run.duration_ms as i64)
   109→        .bind(&run.output)
   110→        .bind(run.input_tokens.map(|v| v as i64))
   111→        .bind(run.output_tokens.map(|v| v as i64))
   112→        .execute(&self.pool)
   113→        .await?;
   114→        Ok(())
   115→    }
   116→
   117→    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {
   118→        let rows = sqlx::query(
   119→            "SELECT job_id, started_at_ms, finished_at_ms, status, error, duration_ms, output, input_tokens, output_tokens
   120→             FROM cron_runs
   121→             WHERE job_id = ?
   122→             ORDER BY started_at_ms DESC
   123→             LIMIT ?",
   124→        )
   125→        .bind(job_id)
   126→        .bind(limit as i64)
   127→        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
   128→        .await?;
   129→
   130→        let mut runs = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());
   131→        for row in rows {
   132→            let status_str: String = row.get("status");
   133→            let status = serde_json::from_str(&status_str)?;
   134→            runs.push(CronRunRecord {
   135→                job_id: row.get("job_id"),
   136→                started_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>("started_at_ms") as u64,
   137→                finished_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>("finished_at_ms") as u64,
   138→                status,
   139→                error: row.get("error"),
   140→                duration_ms: row.get::<i64, _>("duration_ms") as u64,
   141→                output: row.get("output"),
   142→                input_tokens: row
   143→                    .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>("input_tokens")
   144→                    .ok()
   145→                    .flatten()
   146→                    .map(|v| v as u64),
   147→                output_tokens: row
   148→                    .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>("output_tokens")
   149→                    .ok()
   150→                    .flatten()
   151→                    .map(|v| v as u64),
   152→            });
   153→        }
   154→        // Reverse so oldest first (consistent with other stores).
   155→        runs.reverse();
   156→        Ok(runs)
   157→    }
   158→}
   159→
   160→#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]
   161→#[cfg(test)]
   162→mod tests {
   163→    use {super::*, crate::types::*};
   164→
   165→    async fn make_store() -> SqliteStore {
   166→        SqliteStore::new("sqlite::memory:").await.unwrap()
   167→    }
   168→
   169→    fn make_job(id: &str) -> CronJob {
   170→        CronJob {
   171→            id: id.into(),
   172→            name: format!("job-{id}"),
   173→            enabled: true,
   174→            delete_after_run: false,
   175→            schedule: CronSchedule::At { at_ms: 1000 },
   176→            payload: CronPayload::SystemEvent { text: "hi".into() },
   177→            session_target: SessionTarget::Main,
   178→            state: CronJobState::default(),
   179→            sandbox: CronSandboxConfig::default(),
   180→            wake_mode: CronWakeMode::default(),
   181→            system: false,
   182→            created_at_ms: 1000,
   183→            updated_at_ms: 1000,
   184→        }
   185→    }
   186→
   187→    #[tokio::test]
   188→    async fn test_sqlite_roundtrip() {
   189→        let store = make_store().await;
   190→        store.save_job(&make_job("1")).await.unwrap();
   191→        store.save_job(&make_job("2")).await.unwrap();
   192→
   193→        let jobs = store.load_jobs().await.unwrap();
   194→        assert_eq!(jobs.len(), 2);
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    #[tokio::test]
   198→    async fn test_sqlite_upsert() {
   199→        let store = make_store().await;
   200→        store.save_job(&make_job("1")).await.unwrap();
   201→
   202→        let mut job = make_job("1");
   203→        job.name = "updated".into();
   204→        store.save_job(&job).await.unwrap();
   205→
   206→        let jobs = store.load_jobs().await.unwrap();
   207→        assert_eq!(jobs.len(), 1);
   208→        assert_eq!(jobs[0].name, "updated");
   209→    }
   210→
   211→    #[tokio::test]
   212→    async fn test_sqlite_delete() {
   213→        let store = make_store().await;
   214→        store.save_job(&make_job("1")).await.unwrap();
   215→        store.delete_job("1").await.unwrap();
   216→        assert!(store.load_jobs().await.unwrap().is_empty());
   217→    }
   218→
   219→    #[tokio::test]
   220→    async fn test_sqlite_delete_not_found() {
   221→        let store = make_store().await;
   222→        assert!(store.delete_job("nope").await.is_err());
   223→    }
   224→
   225→    #[tokio::test]
   226→    async fn test_sqlite_update() {
   227→        let store = make_store().await;
   228→        store.save_job(&make_job("1")).await.unwrap();
   229→
   230→        let mut job = make_job("1");
   231→        job.name = "patched".into();
   232→        store.update_job(&job).await.unwrap();
   233→
   234→        let jobs = store.load_jobs().await.unwrap();
   235→        assert_eq!(jobs[0].name, "patched");
   236→    }
   237→
   238→    #[tokio::test]
   239→    async fn test_sqlite_runs() {
   240→        let store = make_store().await;
   241→        store.save_job(&make_job("j1")).await.unwrap();
   242→
   243→        for i in 0..5 {
   244→            let run = CronRunRecord {
   245→                job_id: "j1".into(),
   246→                started_at_ms: i * 1000,
   247→                finished_at_ms: i * 1000 + 500,
   248→                status: RunStatus::Ok,
   249→                error: None,
   250→                duration_ms: 500,
   251→                output: None,
   252→                input_tokens: None,
   253→                output_tokens: None,
   254→            };
   255→            store.append_run("j1", &run).await.unwrap();
   256→        }
   257→
   258→        let runs = store.get_runs("j1", 3).await.unwrap();
   259→        assert_eq!(runs.len(), 3);
   260→        // Should be the last 3, in chronological order
   261→        assert_eq!(runs[0].started_at_ms, 2000);
   262→        assert_eq!(runs[2].started_at_ms, 4000);
   263→    }
   264→
   265→    #[tokio::test]
   266→    async fn test_sqlite_runs_empty() {
   267→        let store = make_store().await;
   268→        let runs = store.get_runs("none", 10).await.unwrap();
   269→        assert!(runs.is_empty());
   270→    }
   271→}

    80→    /// Check if a new job can be created. Returns Ok(()) if allowed, Err if rate limited.
    81→    fn check(&mut self) -> Result<()> {
    82→        let now = now_ms();
    83→        let cutoff = now.saturating_sub(self.config.window_ms);
    84→
    85→        // Remove expired timestamps.
    86→        while self.timestamps.front().is_some_and(|&ts| ts < cutoff) {
    87→            self.timestamps.pop_front();
    88→        }
    89→
    90→        if self.timestamps.len() >= self.config.max_per_window {
    91→            return Err(Error::message(format!(
    92→                "rate limit exceeded: max {} jobs per {} seconds",
    93→                self.config.max_per_window,
    94→                self.config.window_ms / 1000
    95→            )));
    96→        }
    97→
    98→        // Record this attempt.
    99→        self.timestamps.push_back(now);
   100→        Ok(())
   101→    }
   102→}

   531→    async fn execute_job(self: &Arc<Self>, job: &CronJob) {
   532→        let started = now_ms();
   533→        info!(id = %job.id, name = %job.name, "executing cron job");
   534→
   535→        #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]
   536→        counter!(cron_metrics::EXECUTIONS_TOTAL).increment(1);
   537→
   538→        // running_at_ms was already set in process_due_jobs() before spawning.
   539→
   540→        let result = match &job.payload {
   541→            CronPayload::SystemEvent { text } => {
   542→                (self.on_system_event)(text.clone());
   543→                Ok(AgentTurnResult {
   544→                    output: "system event injected".to_string(),
   545→                    input_tokens: None,
   546→                    output_tokens: None,
   547→                })
   548→            },
   549→            CronPayload::AgentTurn {
   550→                message,
   551→                model,
   552→                timeout_secs,
   553→                deliver,
   554→                channel,
   555→                to,
   556→            } => {
   557→                let req = AgentTurnRequest {
   558→                    message: message.clone(),
   559→                    model: model.clone(),
   560→                    timeout_secs: *timeout_secs,
   561→                    deliver: *deliver,
   562→                    channel: channel.clone(),
   563→                    to: to.clone(),
   564→                    session_target: job.session_target.clone(),
   565→                    sandbox: job.sandbox.clone(),
   566→                };
   567→                (self.on_agent_turn)(req).await
   568→            },
   569→        };
   570→
   571→        let finished = now_ms();
   572→        let duration_ms = finished - started;
   573→        let (status, error_msg, output, input_tokens, output_tokens) = match &result {
   574→            Ok(r) => {
   575→                #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]
   576→                {
   577→                    if let Some(input) = r.input_tokens {
   578→                        counter!(cron_metrics::INPUT_TOKENS_TOTAL).increment(input);
   579→                    }
   580→                    if let Some(output) = r.output_tokens {
   581→                        counter!(cron_metrics::OUTPUT_TOKENS_TOTAL).increment(output);
   582→                    }
   583→                }
   584→                (
   585→                    RunStatus::Ok,
   586→                    None,
   587→                    Some(r.output.clone()),
   588→                    r.input_tokens,
   589→                    r.output_tokens,
   590→                )
   591→            },
   592→            Err(e) => {
   593→                error!(id = %job.id, error = %e, "cron job failed");
   594→                #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]
   595→                counter!(cron_metrics::ERRORS_TOTAL).increment(1);
   596→                (RunStatus::Error, Some(e.to_string()), None, None, None)
   597→            },
   598→        };
   599→
   600→        #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]
   601→        histogram!(cron_metrics::EXECUTION_DURATION_SECONDS).record(duration_ms as f64 / 1000.0);
   602→
   603→        // Record run.
   604→        let run = CronRunRecord {
   605→            job_id: job.id.clone(),
   606→            started_at_ms: started,
   607→            finished_at_ms: finished,
   608→            status,
   609→            error: error_msg.clone(),
   610→            duration_ms,
   611→            output,
   612→            input_tokens,
   613→            output_tokens,
   614→        };
   615→        if let Err(e) = self.store.append_run(&job.id, &run).await {
   616→            warn!(error = %e, "failed to record cron run");
   617→        }
   618→
   619→        // Update job state.
   620→        let now = now_ms();
   621→        let next_run = compute_next_run(&job.schedule, now).unwrap_or(None);
   622→
   623→        self.update_job_state(&job.id, |state| {
   624→            state.running_at_ms = None;
   625→            state.last_run_at_ms = Some(finished);
   626→            state.last_status = Some(status);
   627→            state.last_error = error_msg;
   628→            state.last_duration_ms = Some(duration_ms);
   629→            state.next_run_at_ms = next_run;
   630→        })
   631→        .await;
   632→
   633→        // Handle one-shot jobs.
   634→        if next_run.is_none() {
   635→            if job.delete_after_run {
   636→                let _ = self.remove(&job.id).await;
   637→                info!(id = %job.id, "one-shot job deleted after run");
   638→            } else {
   639→                // Disable it.
   640→                let mut jobs = self.jobs.write().await;
   641→                if let Some(j) = jobs.iter_mut().find(|j| j.id == job.id) {
   642→                    j.enabled = false;
   643→                    let _ = self.store.update_job(j).await;
   644→                }
   645→            }
   646→        } else {
   647→            // Persist updated state.
   648→            let jobs = self.jobs.read().await;
   649→            if let Some(j) = jobs.iter().find(|j| j.id == job.id) {
   650→                let _ = self.store.update_job(j).await;

     1→//! JSON file-backed cron store with atomic writes.
     2→
     3→use std::path::PathBuf;
     4→
     5→use {
     6→    async_trait::async_trait,
     7→    tokio::{fs, io::AsyncWriteExt},
     8→};
     9→
    10→use crate::{
    11→    Error, Result,
    12→    store::CronStore,
    13→    types::{CronJob, CronRunRecord},
    14→};
    15→
    16→/// File-backed store. Jobs in a single JSON file, runs as JSONL per job.
    17→pub struct FileStore {
    18→    jobs_path: PathBuf,
    19→    runs_dir: PathBuf,
    20→}
    21→
    22→impl FileStore {
    23→    pub fn new(jobs_path: PathBuf, runs_dir: PathBuf) -> Self {
    24→        Self {
    25→            jobs_path,
    26→            runs_dir,
    27→        }
    28→    }
    29→
    30→    /// Create a store using the default `<data_dir>/cron/` layout.
    31→    pub fn default_path() -> Result<Self> {
    32→        let base = moltis_config::data_dir().join("cron");
    33→        Ok(Self::new(base.join("jobs.json"), base.join("runs")))
    34→    }
    35→
    36→    async fn ensure_dirs(&self) -> Result<()> {
    37→        if let Some(parent) = self.jobs_path.parent() {
    38→            fs::create_dir_all(parent).await?;
    39→        }
    40→        fs::create_dir_all(&self.runs_dir).await?;
    41→        Ok(())
    42→    }
    43→
    44→    /// Atomic write: write to temp, rename over target, keep `.bak`.
    45→    async fn atomic_write_jobs(&self, jobs: &[CronJob]) -> Result<()> {
    46→        self.ensure_dirs().await?;
    47→        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(jobs)?;
    48→        let tmp = self.jobs_path.with_extension("json.tmp");
    49→
    50→        fs::write(&tmp, json.as_bytes()).await?;
    51→
    52→        // Backup existing file.
    53→        if fs::try_exists(&self.jobs_path).await.unwrap_or(false) {
    54→            let bak = self.jobs_path.with_extension("json.bak");
    55→            let _ = fs::rename(&self.jobs_path, &bak).await;
    56→        }
    57→
    58→        fs::rename(&tmp, &self.jobs_path).await?;
    59→        Ok(())
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    fn runs_path(&self, job_id: &str) -> PathBuf {
    63→        self.runs_dir.join(format!("{job_id}.jsonl"))
    64→    }
    65→}
    66→
    67→#[async_trait]
    68→impl CronStore for FileStore {
    69→    async fn load_jobs(&self) -> Result<Vec<CronJob>> {
    70→        if !fs::try_exists(&self.jobs_path).await.unwrap_or(false) {
    71→            return Ok(Vec::new());
    72→        }
    73→        let data = fs::read_to_string(&self.jobs_path).await?;
    74→        let jobs: Vec<CronJob> = serde_json::from_str(&data)
    75→            .map_err(|source| Error::external("failed to parse jobs.json", source))?;
    76→        Ok(jobs)
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    async fn save_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
    80→        let mut jobs = self.load_jobs().await?;
    81→        // Replace existing or append.
    82→        if let Some(pos) = jobs.iter().position(|j| j.id == job.id) {
    83→            jobs[pos] = job.clone();
    84→        } else {
    85→            jobs.push(job.clone());
    86→        }
    87→        self.atomic_write_jobs(&jobs).await
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    async fn delete_job(&self, id: &str) -> Result<()> {
    91→        let mut jobs = self.load_jobs().await?;
    92→        let before = jobs.len();
    93→        jobs.retain(|j| j.id != id);
    94→        if jobs.len() == before {
    95→            return Err(Error::job_not_found(id));
    96→        }
    97→        self.atomic_write_jobs(&jobs).await
    98→    }
    99→
   100→    async fn update_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
   101→        let mut jobs = self.load_jobs().await?;
   102→        let pos = jobs
   103→            .iter()
   104→            .position(|j| j.id == job.id)
   105→            .ok_or_else(|| Error::job_not_found(job.id.clone()))?;
   106→        jobs[pos] = job.clone();
   107→        self.atomic_write_jobs(&jobs).await
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    async fn append_run(&self, job_id: &str, run: &CronRunRecord) -> Result<()> {
   111→        self.ensure_dirs().await?;
   112→        let path = self.runs_path(job_id);
   113→        let mut line = serde_json::to_string(run)?;
   114→        line.push('\n');
   115→        let mut file = fs::OpenOptions::new()
   116→            .create(true)
   117→            .append(true)
   118→            .open(&path)
   119→            .await?;
   120→        file.write_all(line.as_bytes()).await?;
   121→        file.flush().await?;
   122→        file.sync_data().await?;
   123→        Ok(())
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {
   127→        let path = self.runs_path(job_id);
   128→        if !fs::try_exists(&path).await.unwrap_or(false) {
   129→            return Ok(Vec::new());
   130→        }
   131→        let data = fs::read_to_string(&path).await?;
   132→        let mut all = Vec::new();
   133→        for (line_no, line) in data.lines().enumerate() {
   134→            if line.trim().is_empty() {
   135→                continue;
   136→            }
   137→            let run = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|source| {
   138→                Error::external(
   139→                    format!(
   140→                        "failed to parse run record in {} at line {}",
   141→                        path.display(),
   142→                        line_no + 1
   143→                    ),
   144→                    source,
   145→                )
   146→            })?;
   147→            all.push(run);
   148→        }
   149→        let start = all.len().saturating_sub(limit);
   150→        Ok(all[start..].to_vec())
   151→    }
   152→}

     1→//! In-memory store for testing.
     2→
     3→use std::{collections::HashMap, sync::Mutex};
     4→
     5→use async_trait::async_trait;
     6→
     7→use crate::{
     8→    Error, Result,
     9→    store::CronStore,
    10→    types::{CronJob, CronRunRecord},
    11→};
    12→
    13→/// In-memory store backed by `HashMap`. No persistence — for tests only.
    14→pub struct InMemoryStore {
    15→    jobs: Mutex<HashMap<String, CronJob>>,
    16→    runs: Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<CronRunRecord>>>,
    17→}
    18→
    19→impl InMemoryStore {
    20→    pub fn new() -> Self {
    21→        Self {
    22→            jobs: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
    23→            runs: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
    24→        }
    25→    }
    26→}
    27→
    28→impl Default for InMemoryStore {
    29→    fn default() -> Self {
    30→        Self::new()
    31→    }
    32→}
    33→
    34→#[async_trait]
    35→impl CronStore for InMemoryStore {
    36→    async fn load_jobs(&self) -> Result<Vec<CronJob>> {
    37→        let jobs = self.jobs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    38→        Ok(jobs.values().cloned().collect())
    39→    }
    40→
    41→    async fn save_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
    42→        let mut jobs = self.jobs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    43→        jobs.insert(job.id.clone(), job.clone());
    44→        Ok(())
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    async fn delete_job(&self, id: &str) -> Result<()> {
    48→        let mut jobs = self.jobs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    49→        if jobs.remove(id).is_none() {
    50→            return Err(Error::job_not_found(id));
    51→        }
    52→        Ok(())
    53→    }
    54→
    55→    async fn update_job(&self, job: &CronJob) -> Result<()> {
    56→        let mut jobs = self.jobs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    57→        if !jobs.contains_key(&job.id) {
    58→            return Err(Error::job_not_found(job.id.clone()));
    59→        }
    60→        jobs.insert(job.id.clone(), job.clone());
    61→        Ok(())
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    async fn append_run(&self, job_id: &str, run: &CronRunRecord) -> Result<()> {
    65→        let mut runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    66→        runs.entry(job_id.to_string())
    67→            .or_default()
    68→            .push(run.clone());
    69→        Ok(())
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {
    73→        let runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    74→        let records = runs.get(job_id).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
    75→        // Return the most recent `limit` entries.
    76→        let start = records.len().saturating_sub(limit);
    77→        Ok(records[start..].to_vec())
    78→    }
    79→}
    80→
    81→#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]

    94→            session_target: SessionTarget::Main,

    97→            wake_mode: CronWakeMode::default(),
    98→            system: false,

   100→            updated_at_ms: 1000,
   101→        }
   102→    }
   103→

  1900→                }
  1901→            }
  1902→        });
  1903→    });
  1904→
  1905→    // Create the system events queue before the callbacks so it can be shared.
  1906→    let events_queue = moltis_cron::system_events::SystemEventsQueue::new();
  1907→
  1908→    // Agent turn: run an LLM turn in a session determined by the job's session_target.
  1909→    let agent_state = Arc::clone(&deferred_state);
  1910→    let agent_events_queue = Arc::clone(&events_queue);
  1911→    let on_agent_turn: moltis_cron::service::AgentTurnFn = Arc::new(move |req| {
  1912→        let st = Arc::clone(&agent_state);
  1913→        let eq = Arc::clone(&agent_events_queue);
  1914→        Box::pin(async move {
  1915→            let state = st
  1916→                .get()
  1917→                .ok_or_else(|| moltis_cron::Error::message("gateway not ready"))?;
  1918→
  1919→            // OpenClaw-style cost guard: if HEARTBEAT.md exists but is effectively
  1920→            // empty (comments/blank scaffold) and there's no explicit
  1921→            // heartbeat.prompt override, skip the LLM turn entirely.
  1922→            let is_heartbeat_turn = matches!(
  1923→                &req.session_target,
  1924→                moltis_cron::types::SessionTarget::Named(name) if name == "heartbeat"
  1925→            );
  1926→            // Check for pending system events (used to bypass the empty-content guard).
  1927→            let has_pending_events = is_heartbeat_turn && !eq.is_empty().await;
  1928→            if is_heartbeat_turn && !has_pending_events {
  1929→                let hb_cfg = state.inner.read().await.heartbeat_config.clone();
  1930→                let has_prompt_override = hb_cfg
  1931→                    .prompt
  1932→                    .as_deref()
  1933→                    .is_some_and(|p| !p.trim().is_empty());
  1934→                let heartbeat_path = moltis_config::heartbeat_path();
  1935→                let heartbeat_file_exists = heartbeat_path.exists();
  1936→                let heartbeat_md = moltis_config::load_heartbeat_md();
  1937→                if heartbeat_file_exists && heartbeat_md.is_none() && !has_prompt_override {
  1938→                    tracing::info!(
  1939→                        path = %heartbeat_path.display(),
  1940→                        "skipping heartbeat LLM turn: HEARTBEAT.md is empty"
  1941→                    );
  1942→                    return Ok(moltis_cron::service::AgentTurnResult {
  1943→                        output: moltis_cron::heartbeat::HEARTBEAT_OK.to_string(),
  1944→                        input_tokens: None,
  1945→                        output_tokens: None,
  1946→                    });
  1947→                }
  1948→            }
  1949→
  1950→            let chat = state.chat().await;
  1951→            let session_key = match &req.session_target {
  1952→                moltis_cron::types::SessionTarget::Named(name) => {
  1953→                    format!("cron:{name}")
  1954→                },
  1955→                _ => format!("cron:{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()),
  1956→            };
  1957→
  1958→            // Clear session history for named cron sessions before execution
  1959→            // so the run starts fresh but the history remains readable for debugging.
  1960→            if matches!(
  1961→                req.session_target,
  1962→                moltis_cron::types::SessionTarget::Named(_)
  1963→            ) {
  1964→                let _ = chat
  1965→                    .clear(serde_json::json!({ "_session_key": session_key }))
  1966→                    .await;
  1967→            }
  1968→
  1969→            // Apply sandbox overrides for this cron session.
  1970→            if let Some(ref router) = state.sandbox_router {
  1971→                router.set_override(&session_key, req.sandbox.enabled).await;
  1972→                if let Some(ref image) = req.sandbox.image {
  1973→                    router.set_image_override(&session_key, image.clone()).await;
  1974→                } else {
  1975→                    router.remove_image_override(&session_key).await;
  1976→                }
  1977→            }
  1978→
  1979→            let prompt_text = if is_heartbeat_turn {
  1980→                let events = eq.drain().await;
  1981→                if events.is_empty() {
  1982→                    req.message.clone()
  1983→                } else {
  1984→                    tracing::info!(
  1985→                        event_count = events.len(),
  1986→                        "enriching heartbeat prompt with system events"
  1987→                    );
  1988→                    moltis_cron::heartbeat::build_event_enriched_prompt(&events, &req.message)
  1989→                }
  1990→            } else {
  1991→                req.message.clone()
  1992→            };
  1993→
  1994→            // When the output will be delivered to a channel, prepend a
  1995→            // formatting hint so the LLM produces channel-friendly content.
  1996→            let prompt_text = if req.deliver && !is_heartbeat_turn {
  1997→                format!(
  1998→                    "Your response will be delivered to an external chat channel. \
  1999→                     Keep it concise and prefer plain text with minimal formatting.\n\n\
  2000→                     {prompt_text}"
  2001→                )
  2002→            } else {
  2003→                prompt_text
  2004→            };
  2005→
  2006→            let mut params = serde_json::json!({
  2007→                "text": prompt_text,
  2008→                "_session_key": session_key,
  2009→            });
  2010→            if let Some(ref model) = req.model {

     1→//! Default configuration template with all options documented.
     2→//!
     3→//! This template is used when creating a new config file. It includes all
     4→//! available options with descriptions, allowing users to see everything
     5→//! that can be configured even if they don't change the defaults.
     6→
     7→/// Generate the default config template with a specific port.
     8→pub fn default_config_template(port: u16) -> String {
     9→    format!(
    10→        r##"# Moltis Configuration
    11→# ====================
    12→# This file contains all available configuration options.
    13→# Uncomment and modify settings as needed.
    14→# Changes require a restart to take effect.
    15→#
    16→# Environment variable substitution is supported: ${{ENV_VAR}}
    17→# Example: api_key = "${{ANTHROPIC_API_KEY}}"
    18→
    19→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    20→# SERVER
    21→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    22→
    23→[server]
    24→bind = "127.0.0.1"                # Address to bind to ("0.0.0.0" for all interfaces)
    25→port = {port}                           # Port number (auto-generated for this installation)
    26→http_request_logs = false              # Enable verbose Axum HTTP request/response logs (debugging)
    27→ws_request_logs = false                # Enable WebSocket RPC request/response logs (debugging)
    28→update_releases_url = "https://www.moltis.org/releases.json"    # Releases manifest URL for update checks (override to use a custom URL)
    29→
    30→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    31→# UPSTREAM PROXY
    32→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    33→# Route all outbound traffic (providers, channels, tools, OAuth) through a

  2032→            let delivery_text = if is_heartbeat_turn {

  2034→                moltis_cron::heartbeat::strip_heartbeat_token(

  2045→                .await;
  2046→

  2049→                input_tokens,

  2052→        })
  2053→    });
  2054→
  2055→    // Build cron notification callback that broadcasts job changes.
  2056→    let deferred_for_cron = Arc::clone(&deferred_state);
  2057→    let on_cron_notify: moltis_cron::service::NotifyFn =
  2058→        Arc::new(move |notification: moltis_cron::types::CronNotification| {
  2059→            let state_opt = deferred_for_cron.get();
  2060→            let Some(state) = state_opt else {
  2061→                return;
  2062→            };
  2063→            let (event, payload) = match &notification {
  2064→                moltis_cron::types::CronNotification::Created { job } => {
  2065→                    ("cron.job.created", serde_json::json!({ "job": job }))
  2066→                },
  2067→                moltis_cron::types::CronNotification::Updated { job } => {
  2068→                    ("cron.job.updated", serde_json::json!({ "job": job }))
  2069→                },
  2070→                moltis_cron::types::CronNotification::Removed { job_id } => {
  2071→                    ("cron.job.removed", serde_json::json!({ "jobId": job_id }))
  2072→                },
  2073→            };
  2074→            // Spawn async broadcast in a background task since we're in a sync callback.
  2075→            let state = Arc::clone(state);
  2076→            tokio::spawn(async move {
  2077→                broadcast(&state, event, payload, BroadcastOpts {
  2078→                    drop_if_slow: true,
  2079→                    ..Default::default()
  2080→                })
  2081→                .await;
  2082→            });
  2083→        });
  2084→
  2085→    // Build rate limit config from moltis config.
  2086→    let rate_limit_config = moltis_cron::service::RateLimitConfig {
  2087→        max_per_window: config.cron.rate_limit_max,
  2088→        window_ms: config.cron.rate_limit_window_secs * 1000,
  2089→    };
  2090→
  2091→    let cron_service = moltis_cron::service::CronService::with_events_queue(
  2092→        cron_store,
  2093→        on_system_event,
  2094→        on_agent_turn,
  2095→        Some(on_cron_notify),
  2096→        rate_limit_config,
  2097→        events_queue,
  2098→    );
  2099→
  2100→    // Wire cron into gateway services.
  2101→    let live_cron = Arc::new(crate::cron::LiveCronService::new(Arc::clone(&cron_service)));
  2102→    services = services.with_cron(live_cron);
  2103→
  2104→    // Build sandbox router from config (shared across sessions).
  2105→    let mut sandbox_config = moltis_tools::sandbox::SandboxConfig::from(&config.tools.exec.sandbox);
  2106→    sandbox_config.container_prefix = Some(sandbox_container_prefix);
  2107→    sandbox_config.timezone = config
  2108→        .user
  2109→        .timezone
  2110→        .as_ref()
  2111→        .map(|tz| tz.name().to_string());
  2112→    let sandbox_router = Arc::new(moltis_tools::sandbox::SandboxRouter::new(
  2113→        sandbox_config.clone(),
  2114→    ));

  "new_string": "/// Cron scheduler configuration.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(default)]\npub struct CronConfig {\n    /// Maximum number of jobs that can be created within the rate limit window.\n    /// Defaults to 10.\n    pub rate_limit_max: usize,\n    /// Rate limit window in seconds. Defaults to 60 (1 minute).\n    pub rate_limit_window_secs: u64,\n    /// Number of days to retain cron session data before auto-cleanup.\n    /// Set to `None` (or 0) to disable retention pruning. Defaults to 7 days.\n    pub session_retention_days: Option<u64>,\n    /// Whether to auto-prune sandbox containers after cron job completion.\n    /// Per-job `auto_prune_container` overrides this global default.\n    /// Defaults to true.\n    #[serde(default = \"default_true\")]\n    pub auto_prune_cron_containers: bool,\n}\n\nfn default_true() -> bool {\n    true\n}\n\nimpl Default for CronConfig {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            rate_limit_max: 10,\n            rate_limit_window_secs: 60,\n            session_retention_days: Some(7),\n            auto_prune_cron_containers: true,\n        }\n    }\n}"

  "new_string": "    async fn append_run(&self, job_id: &str, run: &CronRunRecord) -> Result<()> {\n        let status = serde_json::to_string(&run.status)?;\n        sqlx::query(\n            \"INSERT INTO cron_runs (job_id, started_at_ms, finished_at_ms, status, error, duration_ms, output, input_tokens, output_tokens, session_key)\n             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        )\n        .bind(job_id)\n        .bind(run.started_at_ms as i64)\n        .bind(run.finished_at_ms as i64)\n        .bind(&status)\n        .bind(&run.error)\n        .bind(run.duration_ms as i64)\n        .bind(&run.output)\n        .bind(run.input_tokens.map(|v| v as i64))\n        .bind(run.output_tokens.map(|v| v as i64))\n        .bind(&run.session_key)\n        .execute(&self.pool)\n        .await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs",
  "old_string": "    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {\n        let rows = sqlx::query(\n            \"SELECT job_id, started_at_ms, finished_at_ms, status, error, duration_ms, output, input_tokens, output_tokens\n             FROM cron_runs\n             WHERE job_id = ?\n             ORDER BY started_at_ms DESC\n             LIMIT ?\",\n        )\n        .bind(job_id)\n        .bind(limit as i64)\n        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n        .await?;\n\n        let mut runs = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());\n        for row in rows {\n            let status_str: String = row.get(\"status\");\n            let status = serde_json::from_str(&status_str)?;\n            runs.push(CronRunRecord {\n                job_id: row.get(\"job_id\"),\n                started_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"started_at_ms\") as u64,\n                finished_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"finished_at_ms\") as u64,\n                status,\n                error: row.get(\"error\"),\n                duration_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"duration_ms\") as u64,\n                output: row.get(\"output\"),\n                input_tokens: row\n                    .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>(\"input_tokens\")\n                    .ok()\n                    .flatten()\n                    .map(|v| v as u64),\n                output_tokens: row\n                    .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>(\"output_tokens\")\n                    .ok()\n                    .flatten()\n                    .map(|v| v as u64),\n            });\n        }\n        // Reverse so oldest first (consistent with other stores).\n        runs.reverse();\n        Ok(runs)\n    }",
  "new_string": "    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {\n        let rows = sqlx::query(\n            \"SELECT job_id, started_at_ms, finished_at_ms, status, error, duration_ms, output, input_tokens, output_tokens, session_key\n             FROM cron_runs\n             WHERE job_id = ?\n             ORDER BY started_at_ms DESC\n             LIMIT ?\",\n        )\n        .bind(job_id)\n        .bind(limit as i64)\n        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n        .await?;\n\n        let mut runs = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());\n        for row in runs_to_records(&rows)? {\n            runs.push(row);\n        }\n        // Reverse so oldest first (consistent with other stores).\n        runs.reverse();\n        Ok(runs)\n    }\n\n    async fn prune_runs_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<u64> {\n        let result =\n            sqlx::query(\"DELETE FROM cron_runs WHERE started_at_ms < ?\")\n                .bind(before_ms as i64)\n                .execute(&self.pool)\n                .await?;\n        Ok(result.rows_affected())\n    }\n\n    async fn list_session_keys_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let rows = sqlx::query(\n            \"SELECT DISTINCT session_key FROM cron_runs WHERE session_key IS NOT NULL AND started_at_ms < ?\",\n        )\n        .bind(before_ms as i64)\n        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n        .await?;\n        Ok(rows.iter().map(|r| r.get(\"session_key\")).collect())\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "/// Convert raw SQLite rows into `CronRunRecord` values.\nfn runs_to_records(rows: &[sqlx::sqlite::SqliteRow]) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {\n    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());\n    for row in rows {\n        let status_str: String = row.get(\"status\");\n        let status = serde_json::from_str(&status_str)?;\n        out.push(CronRunRecord {\n            job_id: row.get(\"job_id\"),\n            started_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"started_at_ms\") as u64,\n            finished_at_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"finished_at_ms\") as u64,\n            status,\n            error: row.get(\"error\"),\n            duration_ms: row.get::<i64, _>(\"duration_ms\") as u64,\n            output: row.get(\"output\"),\n            input_tokens: row\n                .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>(\"input_tokens\")\n                .ok()\n                .flatten()\n                .map(|v| v as u64),\n            output_tokens: row\n                .try_get::<Option<i64>, _>(\"output_tokens\")\n                .ok()\n                .flatten()\n                .map(|v| v as u64),\n            session_key: row\n                .try_get::<Option<String>, _>(\"session_key\")\n                .ok()\n                .flatten(),\n        });\n    }\n    Ok(out)\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl CronStore for SqliteStore {"
}

  "new_string": "        let start = all.len().saturating_sub(limit);\n        Ok(all[start..].to_vec())\n    }\n\n    async fn prune_runs_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<u64> {\n        let mut pruned = 0u64;\n        let mut dir = fs::read_dir(&self.runs_dir).await?;\n        while let Some(entry) = dir.next_entry().await? {\n            let path = entry.path();\n            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some(\"jsonl\") {\n                continue;\n            }\n            let data = fs::read_to_string(&path).await?;\n            let mut kept = Vec::new();\n            for line in data.lines() {\n                if line.trim().is_empty() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if let Ok(rec) = serde_json::from_str::<CronRunRecord>(line) {\n                    if rec.started_at_ms < before_ms {\n                        pruned += 1;\n                    } else {\n                        kept.push(line.to_string());\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    kept.push(line.to_string());\n                }\n            }\n            if kept.is_empty() {\n                fs::remove_file(&path).await?;\n            } else {\n                let mut content = kept.join(\"\\n\");\n                content.push('\\n');\n                fs::write(&path, content.as_bytes()).await?;\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(pruned)\n    }\n\n    async fn list_session_keys_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let mut keys = Vec::new();\n        let mut dir = fs::read_dir(&self.runs_dir).await?;\n        while let Some(entry) = dir.next_entry().await? {\n            let path = entry.path();\n            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some(\"jsonl\") {\n                continue;\n            }\n            let data = fs::read_to_string(&path).await?;\n            for line in data.lines() {\n                if line.trim().is_empty() {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if let Ok(rec) = serde_json::from_str::<CronRunRecord>(line) {\n                    if rec.started_at_ms < before_ms {\n                        if let Some(key) = rec.session_key {\n                            if !keys.contains(&key) {\n                                keys.push(key);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(keys)\n    }\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/mahogany-night/crates/cron/src/store_memory.rs",
  "old_string": "    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {\n        let runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n        let records = runs.get(job_id).cloned().unwrap_or_default();\n        // Return the most recent `limit` entries.\n        let start = records.len().saturating_sub(limit);\n        Ok(records[start..].to_vec())\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    async fn get_runs(&self, job_id: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<CronRunRecord>> {\n        let runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n        let records = runs.get(job_id).cloned().unwrap_or_default();\n        // Return the most recent `limit` entries.\n        let start = records.len().saturating_sub(limit);\n        Ok(records[start..].to_vec())\n    }\n\n    async fn prune_runs_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<u64> {\n        let mut runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n        let mut pruned = 0u64;\n        for records in runs.values_mut() {\n            let before_len = records.len();\n            records.retain(|r| r.started_at_ms >= before_ms);\n            pruned += (before_len - records.len()) as u64;\n        }\n        Ok(pruned)\n    }\n\n    async fn list_session_keys_before(&self, before_ms: u64) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n        let mut keys = Vec::new();\n        for records in runs.values() {\n            for rec in records {\n                if rec.started_at_ms < before_ms {\n                    if let Some(ref key) = rec.session_key {\n                        if !keys.contains(key) {\n                            keys.push(key.clone());\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(keys)\n    }\n}"
}

Arc::clone(&cron_store_for_pruning);\n            let prune_session_store = Arc::clone(&session_store);\n            let prune_session_metadata = Arc::clone(&session_metadata);\n            let prune_sandbox = Arc::clone(&sandbox_router);\n            tokio::spawn(async move {\n                let interval = std::time::Duration::from_secs(60 * 60); // hourly\n                loop {\n                    tokio::time::sleep(interval).await;\n                    let retention_ms = retention_days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n                    let cutoff_ms = std::time::SystemTime::now()\n                        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)\n                        .unwrap_or_default()\n                        .as_millis() as u64;\n                    let before_ms = cutoff_ms.saturating_sub(retention_ms);\n\n                    // Collect session keys from old runs before pruning.\n                    let session_keys = match prune_store.list_session_keys_before(before_ms).await {\n                        Ok(keys) => keys,\n                        Err(e) => {\n                            tracing::debug!(error = %e, \"cron session pruning: failed to list session keys\");\n                            Vec::new()\n                        },\n                    };\n\n                    // Clean up sessions and their sandbox containers.\n                    let mut cleaned = 0u64;\n                    for key in &session_keys {\n                        // Skip named cron sessions (they are reused).\n                        if !key.starts_with(\"cron:\") {\n                            continue;\n                        }\n                        // Clear session file.\n                        if let Err(e) = prune_session_store.clear(key).await {\n                            tracing::debug!(key, error = %e, \"cron prune: failed to clear session\");\n                        }\n                        // Remove session metadata.\n                        prune_session_metadata.remove(key).await;\n                        // Clean up sandbox container.\n                        if let Err(e) = prune_sandbox.cleanup_session(key).await {\n                            tracing::debug!(key, error = %e, \"cron prune: sandbox cleanup failed\");\n                        }\n                        cleaned += 1;\n                    }\n\n                    // Prune old run records.\n                    match prune_store.prune_runs_before(before_ms).await {\n                        Ok(0) => {},\n                        Ok(n) => tracing::info!(\n                            pruned_runs = n,\n                            pruned_sessions = cleaned,\n                            retention_days,\n                            \"cron retention: pruned old runs and sessions\"\n                        ),\n                        Err(e) => tracing::debug!(error = %e, \"cron retention: failed to prune runs\"),\n                    }\n                }\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pre-pull browser container image if browser is enabled and sandbox mode is available.\n    // Browser sandbox mode follows session sandbox mode, so we pre-pull if sandboxing is available."
}
```
