Committed. Here's a summary of everything implemented:

**15 files changed, +477/-31 lines across all 5 phases:**

| Phase | What |
|-------|------|
| 1 - Data model | `session_key` on `CronRunRecord`, `auto_prune_container` on `CronSandboxConfig`, `session_retention_days` + `auto_prune_cron_containers` on `CronConfig`, SQLite migration, schema map, config template |
| 2 - Threading | Gateway's `on_agent_turn` returns `session_key` in `AgentTurnResult`, `execute_job` persists it in run records |
| 3 - Container prune | After `send_sync`, calls `SandboxRouter::cleanup_session` when `auto_prune_container` resolves to true (per-job overrides global) |
| 4 - Session retention | `prune_runs_before` + `list_session_keys_before` on all 3 store backends, hourly background task in gateway deletes expired sessions/metadata/containers |
| 5 - Tests | Serde roundtrip, backward compat, `session_key` storage, `prune_runs_before` for SQLite/memory stores, `list_session_keys_before` |

All 644 tests pass, clippy clean, fmt clean.

> AGENT

Two valid review comments:

1. **P1**: Named-session guard is always false — `!key.starts_with("cron:")` is always false since all cron session keys start with `cron:`. Need to check if the suffix is a UUID to distinguish isolated from named sessions.
2. **P2**: Magic epoch math `retention_days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000` violates CLAUDE.md rule. Use `time` crate instead.

Let me fix both.

If they were deleted from the codebase, [click here and archive them to remove them from the performance reports](https://codspeed.io/moltis-org/moltis/branches/mahogany-night?q=is%3Askipped\u0026utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=comment-v2\u0026utm_content=archive).\n","created_at":"2026-04-06T09:20:14Z","id":4191356142,"user":"codspeed-hq[bot]"}
{"body":"\u003ch3\u003eGreptile Summary\u003c/h3\u003e\n\nThis PR implements auto-cleanup of orphaned cron sessions and sandbox containers: a background task prunes run records and sessions older than `session_retention_days` (hourly), and containers are optionally cleaned up immediately after each cron run when `auto_prune_cron_containers` is enabled.\n\n- **P1**: `cleanup_session` — called in both the on-completion auto-prune path and the hourly retention loop — removes the sandbox mode override but does **not** call `remove_image_override`, leaving per-session image override entries in the router's `image_overrides` map indefinitely for any job with a custom `sandbox.image`. The `else if` (non-prune) branch correctly calls both removals, confirming this asymmetry is unintentional.\n\n\u003ch3\u003eConfidence Score: 4/5\u003c/h3\u003e\n\nSafe to merge after fixing the image-override leak in cleanup_session.\n\nOne P1 defect: cleanup_session omits remove_image_override, causing unbounded growth of the image_overrides map for cron jobs using a custom sandbox image. All other pieces — the UUID guard, time-crate retention math, migration, serde compat, and the FileStore implementation — are correct.\n\ncrates/gateway/src/server.rs (both auto-prune call sites) and crates/tools/src/sandbox/router.rs (cleanup_session definition, not in this PR's diff)\n\n\u003ch3\u003eImportant Files Changed\u003c/h3\u003e\n\n\n\n\n| Filename | Overview |\n|----------|----------|\n| crates/gateway/src/server.rs | Adds on-completion container auto-prune and hourly retention task; image override not removed in the auto-prune path (P1) |\n| crates/cron/src/service.rs | execute_job now captures and records session_key from AgentTurnResult; logic is correct |\n| crates/cron/src/types.rs | Adds optional session_key to CronRunRecord and auto_prune_container to CronSandboxConfig with correct serde defaults and backward-compat |\n| crates/cron/src/store_sqlite.rs | Implements prune_runs_before and list_session_keys_before correctly with DISTINCT, NULL guard, and proper index usage |\n| crates/cron/src/store_memory.rs | Correct in-memory implementations of the two new pruning methods; DISTINCT dedup handled manually |\n| crates/cron/src/store_file.rs | Implements list_session_keys_before and prune_runs_before for the JSONL file-backed store; atomic-rewrite approach is correct |\n| crates/config/src/schema.rs | Adds session_retention_days (Option\u003cu64\u003e) and auto_prune_cron_containers (bool, default true) with sensible defaults |\n| crates/cron/migrations/20260406100000_cron_runs_session_key.sql | Adds nullable session_key column and index with IF NOT EXISTS guard — safe for existing databases |\n| crates/cron/src/store.rs | Trait extended with two optional default-no-op methods; correct design for backward compatibility with custom implementations |\n| crates/config/src/validate.rs | schema_map updated to include new cron fields; no issues |\n| crates/config/src/template.rs | Config template updated with commented-out examples for both new fields |\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\n\u003ch3\u003eSequence Diagram\u003c/h3\u003e\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Timer as Retention Timer (hourly)\n    participant Store as CronStore\n    participant Sessions as SessionStore\n    participant Router as SandboxRouter\n\n    Timer-\u003e\u003eStore: list_session_keys_before(cutoff_ms)\n    Store--\u003e\u003eTimer: [\"cron:\u003cuuid1\u003e\", \"cron:\u003cuuid2\u003e\"]\n    loop for each key\n        Timer-\u003e\u003eTimer: verify suffix is valid UUID\n        Timer-\u003e\u003eSessions: clear(key)\n        Timer-\u003e\u003eSessions: remove metadata(key)\n        Timer-\u003e\u003eRouter: cleanup_session(key)\n        Note over Router: removes override ✓\u003cbr/\u003eremoves container ✓\u003cbr/\u003eremoves image_override ✗ P1\n    end\n    Timer-\u003e\u003eStore: prune_runs_before(cutoff_ms)\n    Store--\u003e\u003eTimer: pruned_count\n\n    Note over Router: On-completion path (auto_prune=true)\u003cbr/\u003esame gap — cleanup_session called\u003cbr/\u003ewithout remove_image_override\n```\n\n\u003c!-- greptile_other_comments_section --\u003e\n\n\u003csub\u003eReviews (2): Last reviewed commit: [\"fix(cron): use time crate for retention ...\"](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/commit/750798c5d80982415309683cea88a419fcb027f9) | [Re-trigger Greptile](https://app.greptile.com/api/retrigger?id=27444061)\u003c/sub\u003e","created_at":"2026-04-06T09:21:37Z","id":4191362095,"user":"greptile-apps[bot]"}

> AGENT

One new P1: when `list_session_keys_before` fails, the code falls through to `prune_runs_before` which deletes run records without having cleaned up the associated sessions first — orphaning them. Fix: `continue` the loop iteration instead of falling back to empty vec.
